TL;DR: 本文研究三目标优化中基于积分R2指标的固定基数子集选择问题。正面结果：证明R2改进量是单调子模集函数，贪心选择可达到(1-1/e)的近似保证，并给出O(n^6)的贪心实现算法。负面结果：证明精确固定基数子集选择在三目标情形下是NP-hard问题，通过将问题转化为加权锚定盒并集问题并应用三维锚定盒构造实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限帕累托前沿近似中选取固定数量的代表性点是多目标优化中的基本后处理任务，本文针对三目标情形下积分R2指标的子集选择问题进行系统研究。

Method: 正面：证明积分R2改进量相对于固定基线是单调子模集函数，基于理想点R2指标给出贪心选择的(1-1/e)间隙缩减保证，并实现基于细分的精确积分R2贪心算法(O(n^6))。负面：通过透视变换将Tchebycheff阴影改进映射为加权锚定盒并集问题，再改编Bringmann等人的三维锚定盒构造证明NP-hard性。

Result: 三目标情形下，贪心选择可达到(1-1/e)的近似保证；但精确固定基数子集选择是NP-hard的，将双目标与三目标情形在计算复杂度上区分开来。

Conclusion: 三目标积分R2子集选择问题具有子模可近似性与精确求解NP-hard性的双重性质，为基于子模优化的近似方法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Selecting a fixed number of representative points from a finite Pareto-front approximation is a fundamental post-processing task in multiobjective optimization. This paper studies this problem for the integral R2 indicator in three objectives, where the indicator is defined as the integral of the lower envelope of weighted Tchebycheff scalarizations over the two-dimensional weight simplex. We provide two complementary algorithmic results. On the positive side, we show that the integral R2 improvement with respect to any fixed baseline is a monotone submodular set function. For the usual ideal-point based R2 indicator, with the ideal point fixed, this yields a direct gap-reduction guarantee: greedy selection closes at least a $(1-1/e)$-fraction of the maximum possible R2 gap between a fixed dominated anchor value and the best cardinality-$k$ value. We also give a tested greedy implementation that evaluates exact integral R2 values by subdivision, with worst-case running time $O(n^6)$. On the negative side, we prove that exact fixed-cardinality subset selection is NP-hard already in three objectives. The hardness proof uses a perspective transformation that maps Tchebycheff-shadow improvements to a weighted anchored-box union problem with density $(x_1+x_2+x_3)^{-4}$, and then adapts the three-dimensional anchored-box construction of Bringmann, Cabello, and Emmerich. Together, these results separate the tractable two-objective case from the three-objective case while identifying a principled approximation route based on submodular optimization.

</details>

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of minimizing equivalent carbon emissions in residential photovoltaic-battery energy storage systems (PV-BESS) under uncertainty. We develop and compare a hierarchy of linear optimization models that differ in their degree of anticipativity and feedback complexity, ranging from a rule-based self-consumption heuristic to fully stochastic formulations with linear feedback control. The proposed models explicitly incorporate the stochastic variability of household load, solar production, and grid carbon intensity through large scenario sets generated via principal component analysis of real operational data. Computational experiments on synthetic yet realistic scenarios show that direct stochastic optimization of expected emissions (Programmed Battery model) substantially outperforms heuristic control, achieving emission reductions close to the theoretical lower bound provided by the Omniscient Battery benchmark. Feedback-based models marginally improve training performance but do not generalize better on unseen data, while incurring higher computational costs. Overall, results demonstrate that linear stochastic programming provides an effective and tractable framework for emission-aware energy management in distributed PV-BESS systems.

Abstract: We consider the problem of optimally distributing resources from a set of suppliers to a set of consumers in the presence of general transportation constraints, and including heterogeneous flexibility in the marginal constraints. Our principal contribution in this paper is to consider a generalisation of the classic entropically regularised Optimal Transport formulation in which such problems can be solved with a Sinkhorn algorithm. In particular, we present provably convergent Sinkhorn-like algorithms for solving this class of problems, and provide examples to both illustrate the utility of our approach as well as its efficacy.

Abstract: Multiobjective composite optimization problems arise in sparse regularization, constrained multiobjective models, and multi-task learning, but their numerical solution remains challenging when the smooth components are ill-conditioned. Proximal gradient methods are inexpensive per iteration but may converge slowly, while proximal Newton and quasi-Newton methods exploit curvature information at the cost of evaluating expensive metric proximal mappings. To address these issues, we propose a preconditioned proximal Barzilai--Borwein method for multiobjective composite optimization. The method combines objective-wise Barzilai--Borwein scaling, which reduces imbalance among objectives, with a common preconditioner that captures shared curvature information. To avoid non-diagonal metric proximal mappings, we develop a subspace variant in which the search direction is computed in a two-dimensional subspace generated by a proximal-gradient-type direction and a projected historical direction. By constructing a conjugate basis with respect to the preconditioning metric, the subspace model decomposes into tractable one-dimensional subproblems. The framework is further extended to nonsmooth terms of the form $g_i(Ax)$ through a linear-operator-aware preconditioner, yielding explicit proximal evaluations via dual subproblems. We also analyze an inexact version based on relaxed descent conditions. We establish the global convergence of the inexact algorithm in the nonconvex setting and prove a linear convergence rate under an error-bound condition. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned $\ell_1$-regularized, structured $\ell_1$-regularized, and linearly constrained problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>

Abstract: The outer approximation algorithm is a widely used method for solving convex mixed-integer nonlinear programs. While the algorithm is well established in theory and practice, certain assumptions underlying its convergence are rarely discussed in the literature. In this paper, we examine two such assumptions: that a constraint qualification holds at the optimal solution of each nonlinear programming subproblem, and that these subproblems can be solved exactly. We argue that both assumptions are connected to the issue of cycling, by which we mean that the same integer assignment reappears in successive iterations of the algorithm. When a constraint qualification fails, separation of the current iterate from the feasible set is not guaranteed, which can cause the algorithm to stall. When the nonlinear programming subproblem is solved only approximately, separation may likewise fail, and we show that high precision can be required in certain cases. To formalize the connection between these issues, we prove that when Slater's condition is nearly violated, a point close to the exact solution can be found at which separation fails. Furthermore, within the outer approximation algorithm, we propose to use extended cutting planes as a fallback strategy when cycling is detected. We prove that this approach yields a finitely convergent algorithm under relaxed assumptions regarding constraint qualifications, thereby generalizing the convergence theory of the outer approximation algorithm.

</details>

Abstract: Phylogenetic reconstruction is one of the major challenges in computational biology. Among existing reconstruction methods for phylogenetic networks, an important subtask emerges in extending a leaf-labelling on a phylogenetic network to determine a most parsimonious tree inside the network. There exist different variants of this subtask depending on the biological model assumptions for which distinct evolutionary phenomena are captured by the network. In this article we assume that next to hybridization or recombination events, also allopolyploidy or incomplete lineage sorting are present. Then, finding the most parsimonious tree inside the network is called the parental parsimony score problem (PPS), a NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem. We provide the first constant-factor approximation for the PPS on arbitrary but fixed leaf labels and a class of networks on which the PPS remains NP-hard, namely binary, semi-simplex, tree-child phylogenetic networks. Furthermore, we introduce a novel exact solution algorithm for the PPS on binary, tree-child phylogenetic networks and analyze its performance on simulated data.

</details>

Abstract: Path-dependent McKean--Vlasov (MKV) control models large interacting populations with history-dependent dynamics and costs. This paper develops a unified approximation-and-learning framework for continuous time path-dependent MKV problem under open-loop controls. First, an Euler discretization scheme with piecewise-constant controls is shown to achieve a non-asymptotic error of $O(h^{1/4})$. Second, we establish a discrete dynamic programming principle and prove value equivalence between open-loop and history-dependent feedback controls, enabling optimization on a reduced filtration. Third, an interacting particle system is introduced to approximate the continuous-time value, yielding an overall error bound of $O(h^{1/4}) + O(M^{-γ})$ for $M$ particles and an explicitly given $γ> 0$. Finally, we propose a fully implementable neural-network policy-gradient method using pathwise features. Numerical experiments, including a path-dependent linear-quadratic benchmark, demonstrate the effectiveness of the algorithm.

</details>

Method: 通过建立临界点集合的闭式刻画，推导全局最优性的充要条件，证明每个非最优临界点都存在正曲率方向。对HePPCA，证明其种群版本是QPOC特例，并通过大样本分析证明样本版本高概率继承这些性质。

Result: QPOC的每个临界点要么是全局最大值点要么是严格鞍点；HePPCA种群版本具有良性优化景观，并在每个全局最大值点附近满足局部测地线强凹性；样本版本在大样本下高概率继承这些性质。

Conclusion: QPOC和HePPCA都具有良性优化景观，基于收缩的优化方法可以避免严格鞍点并线性收敛到全局解，理论结果通过数值实验得到验证。

Abstract: In this work, we study the optimization landscape of homogeneous quadratic programs with orthogonality constraints (QPOC) and apply the resulting theory to heteroscedastic probabilistic PCA (HePPCA). For QPOC, we establish a complete characterization of the benign optimization landscape by showing that every critical point is either a global maximizer or a strict saddle point. Our analysis builds on a closed-form characterization of the critical point set, from which we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for global optimality and show that every non-optimal critical point has a direction of positive curvature. As an application, we show that the population version of HePPCA is a special instance of QPOC and therefore has a benign optimization landscape; moreover, it satisfies local geodesic strong concavity near every global maximizer. We furthermore prove that, when the sample size is sufficiently large, the sample version of HePPCA inherits these favorable properties with high probability. Together with existing theory on avoiding strict saddle points, our results provide a theoretical justification for the observed local linear convergence of retraction-based optimization methods to global solutions for both QPOC and HePPCA. Finally, we present numerical experiments to corroborate our theoretical results.

</details>

Abstract: This work introduces an optimization model to support decision-making in the design of integrated transit systems, with the aim of improving urban mobility. We assume that daily user trips consist of two types of segments: short segments where users apply their own transport between their homes or workplaces and the public transit access points, and a longer segment on the transportation network. Users' accessibility is ensured by imposing an upper limit on the travel times of the short segments. We assume that only users within a given coverage radius will employ the system. On the other hand, system effectiveness is ensured by limiting travel time on the transportation network. Two mixed-integer linear programming formulations and a matheuristic procedure are proposed, and their efficiency is evaluated through computational experiments. The results allow us to study the structure of the solutions obtained and analyze the effects of the parameters considered.

</details>

Abstract: The $w$-optimization problem is a mathematical problem abstracted from a useful tool for general data fusion rooted in various engineering tasks. Purely from the perspective of practical applications for this useful tool namely the split covariance intersection filter (Split CIF), it is sufficient to know that the $w$-optimization problem enjoys not only the second-order convexity (namely convexity in conventional sense) but also the fourth-order convexity, thanks to which a guaranteed fast implementation of the Split CIF can be realized. On the other hand, based on certain observations and analysis, the author proposes a conjecture concerning the $w$-optimization problem for further mathematical study. The conjecture is that the $w$-optimization problem has arbitrary even-order convexity, or in other words, it has the second-order convexity, the fourth-order convexity, the sixth-order convexity, and so on -- As mentioned above, whether the $w$-optimization problem has higher-order convexity or not might be of little interest itself for practical applications, yet the author believes that the conjecture would stimulate study on systematic mathematical techniques that are potentially interesting and valuable -- This paper presents the conjecture in details.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes型流体模型的最优控制验证理论长期缺乏关注，特别是对于包含非线性吸收项的Brinkman-Forchheimer系统，其验证定理和反馈刻画需要不同于二维Navier-Stokes方程的数学框架。

Method: 采用强解理论，通过精细处理非线性吸收项、发展负阶Sobolev空间中的新估计以及更强拓扑下的连续依赖性估计，在二维和三维（包括超临界情形r∈(3,5]及临界情形r=3且2βμ≥1）下建立验证定理。

Result: 成功推导了Pontryagin最大值原理，在二维和三维（含超临界和临界情形）下严格证明了验证定理和反馈刻画，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Conclusion: 本文建立的验证框架不仅适用于二维情形，还覆盖了三维修正临界和超临界情形，为更广泛流体控制问题的理论分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: This article establishes a verification theorem for an optimal control problem governed by the two- and three-dimensional convective Brinkman--Forchheimer equations on the $d$-dimensional torus, $d\in\{2,3\}$:
  $$\frac{\partial\mathfrak{u}}{\partial t}
  -μΔ\mathfrak{u}
  +(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\nabla)\mathfrak{u}
  +α\mathfrak{u}
  +β|\mathfrak{u}|^{r-1}\mathfrak{u}
  +\nabla\mathfrak{p}
  =\boldsymbol{f},
  \qquad
  \nabla\cdot\mathfrak{u}=0,$$
  where $μ,α,β>0$ and $r\in[1,\infty)$. We derive the Pontryagin maximum principle and develop a verification framework for the associated control problem, a topic that has received comparatively little attention for fluid models of Navier--Stokes type. A major challenge in establishing the verification theorem and the corresponding feedback characterization for the CBF system is that the analysis requires a substantially different regularity framework from that used for the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations. In particular, the present approach relies on strong solution theory, a delicate treatment of the nonlinear absorption term, novel estimates in negative-order Sobolev spaces, and continuous dependence estimates in stronger topologies, especially in the three-dimensional setting. A distinctive feature of the present work is that the verification framework is developed not only in two dimensions, but also in the three-dimensional supercritical regime, corresponding to $r\in(3,5]$, and in the critical case $r=3$ under the condition $2βμ\geq1$. Consequently, the feedback characterization and verification arguments can be rigorously justified in both two and three dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代数据生成管线，通过隔离随行为线性缩放的特征样本（而非简单二元对比样本）来更好地解耦模型特征，并以消除语言模型中的谄媚行为（sycophancy）为例，证明该方法在特征检测、确定性评分和鲁棒控制方面优于或持平LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，且计算需求更低、可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法依赖二元对比样本对来检测和控制模型行为，但这类样本难以有效解耦特征，导致可解释性框架无法可靠地检测行为对应的模型特征，限制了引导效果。

Method: 提出迭代数据生成管线，通过生成随行为程度线性缩放的多层次样本（而非二元对立样本），隔离出级联的线性特征。以谄媚行为为案例，使用这些级联样本发现线性可分的特征子空间，并选择更清晰对应目标行为的模型激活。

Result: 级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分子空间；在检测、确定性评分和鲁棒引导任务中，该方法匹配或超越LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，同时计算需求更低、可解释性更强。

Conclusion: 超越二元对比样本、引入线性缩放的多层次样本可以更好地解耦行为特征，提升模型行为解释和控制的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [86] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前以准确率为中心的基准评估范式，提出应同时测量构造效度、泛化性、效率、可靠性、模型与支架的相对重要性以及人机协作提升等六个维度的智能体性能。以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，证明在准确率饱和后这些维度仍能提供有意义洞察，并提出了改进的CORE-Bench v1.1、OOD测试集，以及人机协作随机实验发现约两倍的统计显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试在准确率饱和后通常被直接替换，忽视了其他关键维度的性能评估，缺乏对智能体能力的全面理解。

Method: 以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，分析构造效度问题；引入CORE-Bench v1.1和CORE-Bench OOD；测量效率、可靠性、模型与支架性能；开展小规模随机实验衡量人机协作提升。

Result: 准确率饱和后，CORE-Bench v1.1仍可用于测量效率、可靠性、模型性能和支架性能；人机协作实验发现约两倍的统计显著加速（因五分之一纯人类复现超时，该加速可能被低估）。

Conclusion: 应超越以准确率为中心的评估范式，采用多维度评估体系以更全面地理解智能体性能。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [87] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现指令微调模型中的拒绝机制（refusal）受顺从人格（compliant persona）调控。在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct中，顺从人格方向能抑制拒绝（Llama模型拒绝率从97%降至2%），而拒绝方向只在后期层部分恢复拒绝行为。拒绝的生效依赖于人格门控，不能孤立看待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究分别发现了激活空间中的拒绝方向（refusal direction）和人格特征方向（persona direction），但二者被当作独立机制研究。本文旨在揭示二者之间的交互关系，特别是顺从人格如何调控拒绝行为。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct两个模型中提取顺从人格方向和拒绝方向，通过干预（steering）操作观察拒绝率变化，并在不同层（early/late layers）进行投影（projecting out）实验验证门控机制的位置。

Result: 1）顺从人格方向可显著抑制拒绝行为，Llama模型拒绝率从97%降至2%；2）恢复拒绝方向仅在后期层部分恢复拒绝行为，早期层无效；3）在后期层窗口中投影掉人格方向可将拒绝率恢复至基线水平，而投影随机方向无效。

Conclusion: 拒绝机制并非独立运作，而是在后期层表达阶段受人格特征门控。将拒绝视为孤立的单一方向会忽略其对人格的依赖关系。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [88] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlgoEvolve框架，利用LLM作为语义变异算子，通过进化算法生成、评估并迭代改进算法交易策略，并引入元进化外环自动优化提示词，在噪声大、非平稳、高度不连续的金融领域实现了自适应交易逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM驱动的语义进化范式从静态编码基准测试扩展到算法交易这一更具挑战性的领域——该领域具有噪声大、非平稳、高度不连续的特点，现有的静态评测方法无法充分体现LLM在复杂动态环境中的程序合成能力。

Method: 提出AlgoEvolve框架：内环使用LLM作为语义变异算子，对以Python代码表达的交易策略进行进化生成、评估和迭代改进；外环引入元进化机制，自动进化生成引导内环程序合成的提示词，优化搜索启发式策略以平衡探索与利用、减少零交易失败。

Result: 系统展现出涌现性的自适应交易逻辑，能自主切换交易规则；元进化外环发现的提示词启发式策略优于初始人工设计的指令，有效平衡了探索与利用，并减少了零交易失败次数。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义进化是在复杂环境中进行持续程序合成的可行方法，为算法交易等动态领域的自动化策略发现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [89] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的自动化比较分析流程，用于大规模治理话语分析。通过对ERC-8004（无许可、链上）和Google A2A（企业主导）两种智能体互操作性标准的4,323条治理参与记录进行分析，研究发现治理形式影响实质性主题焦点，但两种体制在参与不平等和社区碎片化程度上相当。无许可环境中的话语对齐更密集，表明开放治理可能促进更大的主题趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI智能体协议日益增多，但塑造其互操作性标准的治理结构在实证层面仍缺乏系统研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过大规模、可复现的方法研究治理话语中的社会技术权力结构。

Method: 采用LLM辅助的自动化比较分析流程，整合了自动化标注、神经主题建模和多层网络分析。具体对ERC-8004（无许可链上标准）和Google A2A（企业主导标准）两种治理体制的4,323条参与记录进行分析。

Result: 治理形式影响主题焦点；两种体制的参与不平等和社区碎片化程度相似；无许可环境中的话语对齐更密集，表明开放治理可能促进更强的主题趋同。

Conclusion: LLM辅助方法能够推进技术治理的实证研究，对设计更公平的智能体AI标准具有启示意义。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [90] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合了来自Reddit、WebMD和FDA不良事件报告系统的数据，用于九种抗抑郁药物的安全性信息。通过LLM实体识别管线（药物F1=0.969，疾病F1=0.973），发现患者社区数据与FDA报告存在显著差异，社区来源中许多不良事件在FDA报告前数百天即已出现，最终建立了可追溯、知源的知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者在线寻求药物信息的需求日益增长，但精神科药物的安全性知识分散在权威但抽象的监管不良事件记录与经验贴近但未经验证的患者叙述之间。在精神医学领域，信息整合不当可能放大恐惧、引发反安慰剂效应和用药不依从，因此需要一种既能融合不同来源又不混淆证据与轶事的框架。

Method: 开发了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合了466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论和二十年的FDA FAERS数据（针对九种抗抑郁药）。使用大语言模型实体识别管线（经医师注释基准测试），以ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA术语体系为基础构建Neo4j知识图谱，保留数据来源（provenance）信息。

Result: LLM实体识别管线在药物和疾病识别上分别达到0.969和0.973的最高F1分数。两个患者社区平台之间的一致性（Jaccard相似度最高达0.905）远高于它们与FDA报告的一致性。对于舍曲林，许多不良事件在社区来源中出现的时间比FDA记录早数百天。

Conclusion: 建立了来源感知（source-aware）的数据整合方法，为精神科药物信息提供了更可审计的路径，其实用性和患者获益有待前瞻性检验。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [91] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-Elo评分系统，融合认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型，通过利用棋局中每步走棋的质量信息来替代传统Elo系统仅依赖比赛结果的滞后响应，实现更快速、更精准的棋手技能波动捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评分系统仅依赖比赛结果，存在响应滞后问题，无法捕捉棋手在比赛过程中的技能波动，而引入每步走棋信息又面临噪音大、状态空间庞大的挑战。

Method: 借鉴认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型（DDM），将技能表达建模为决策过程，整合每步走棋级别的数据来捕捉快速技能波动；并给出严格的数学推导，证明DD-Elo与传统Elo系统的偏差有界。

Result: 实验表明DD-Elo在适应技能变化的速度上显著快于传统Elo系统，且具有可解释性、高响应性和向后兼容性。

Conclusion: DD-Elo为国际象棋评分生态系统提供了一种可解释、高响应且向后兼容的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [92] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算治理模型，要求AI代理在执行高风险行动（如临床处方和软件部署）前，必须获得多个独立权威源的加密认证证据，而非监控其推理过程。代理保留规划自主权，但执行权受确定性策略约束，所有决策记录在防篡改日志中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理可能执行不可逆的高风险行动（如临床处方、软件部署），现有方法依赖监控推理过程，但人类制度对强大自主行动者的治理并不依赖监控推理，而是要求行动时有独立认证的证据。作者希望将这一制度模式形式化为AI代理系统的计算治理模型。

Method: 形式化定义了一个计算治理模型：代理保留规划和推理的完全自主权，但对指定的高风险行动没有执行权；执行需满足预设条件，每个条件由独立的权威来源认证，加密绑定到声明的意图，并由确定性策略评估；所有决策记录在可独立重新验证的防篡改日志中。提供了概念验证实现，并用软件部署和临床处方示例说明。

Result: 提出了一个将人类制度中的"独立认证证据"模式形式化的计算治理模型，并实现了概念验证系统，通过软件部署和临床处方两个示例展示了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 该治理模型通过分离自主推理与执行权限，结合独立多方认证和防篡改日志，为高风险AI代理行动提供了一种不依赖内部推理监控的替代治理方案，兼顾了代理的自主性与行动的安全性、可审计性。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [93] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COrigami，一个端到端的AI驱动管道，通过自然语言生成折纸折痕图案，融合了几何约束（平面可折叠性）与主观美学评估，实现了数学上严谨的协同创意设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在可验证解的问题上取得了显著成功，但在生成既满足严格几何约束又符合主观视觉美学的物理艺术品方面仍面临挑战。计算折纸作为一个数学上严谨的环境，将艺术设计锚定在平面可折叠性的方程中，为探索这一挑战提供了理想平台。

Method: COrigami管道包括五个阶段：从自然语言生成语义简笔画、计算基础布局、求解平面可折叠折痕图案、对平面折痕图案进行塑形，以及通过自主美学评估循环驱动的强化学习细化生成模型。

Result: 该系统作为高效的协作助手，能够生成结构化的起点供人类艺术家进一步扩展和塑形，通过整合算法优化与自主美学批评，满足多目标物理约束。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了AI系统如何能够满足多目标物理约束，实现可靠、数学上严谨的协同创造力，证明了在严格几何约束环境下实现AI辅助艺术创作的可行性。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [94] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，随着AI编程智能体的能力增强，验证解决方案比生成解决方案更难，因为验证器只是人类意图的代理。作者从可扩展性、保真度和鲁棒性三个维度刻画验证信号质量，研究了四种奖励构建方式（测试验证器、评分标准验证器、用户即验证器、自动智能体验证器），发现固定奖励函数无法随策略能力增长而保持有效，验证必须与生成器协同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为验证比生成容易，但当前编码智能体的能力提升使得生成复杂候选解变得容易，而可靠验证反而成为更难的问题。验证器只是人类意图的代理，存在意图本身欠指定和优化过程中代理与意图差距扩大（奖励破解、信号饱和）的双重困难。

Method: 从可扩展性、保真度、鲁棒性三个维度刻画验证信号质量，研究四种奖励构建方案：用于通用编码任务的测试验证器、用于前端任务的评分标准验证器、用于真实世界智能体任务的用户即验证器、用于长周期任务的自动智能体验证器。在不同任务类型和策略能力水平下进行深度分析和实验。

Result: 针对性验证设计能有效抑制奖励破解、提升任务完成质量，在多个内部和公开基准上取得显著提升。实验表明，固定奖励函数无法随策略能力持续增长而保持有效。

Conclusion: 验证必须与生成器协同演化（co-evolve），没有固定不变的奖励函数能始终有效。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [95] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对能源市场分析的真实世界工具增强型LLM智能体评估框架，包含243个专家策划的任务、可配置的领域工具套件（实时电力市场API、监管文档检索、关税数据库等）以及多维评估协议，并对闭源和开源LLM进行了比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试主要集中在通用领域和金融、编程、法律、药物发现等专业领域，而能源领域评估仍局限于静态知识回忆。能源行业需要实时数据检索、专业监管和市场知识以及多步定量推理，现有评估方法无法覆盖这一关键缺口。

Method: 构建了包含243个专家策划问题的评估环境，覆盖三个类别：（1）市场数据检索与分析，（2）知识检索与解释，（3）高级量化建模与决策分析。为智能体配备可配置的领域工具套件，包括美国主要ISO实时电力市场API、监管文档检索、公用事业关税数据库、资产优化模型及RAG系统。采用多维评估协议，从方法正确性、答案准确性、属性对齐度和来源有效性四个维度评分，并按类别路由匹配评分标准。

Result: 对闭源和开源LLM进行了比较分析，展示了模型能力与领域工具在高风险专业领域中的交互效果。关键工件已公开发布以支持可重复性和未来研究。

Conclusion: 能源领域的智能体评估需要超越静态知识回忆，本文提出的框架填补了这一空白，为评估LLM智能体在真实能源市场分析任务中的表现提供了标准化的多维度评估方法。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [96] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能力发展迅速，但评估体系严重滞后。现有基准局限于孤立任务，无法测量跨模态信息整合能力。作者通过审视评估现状并梳理基准分类法，识别出时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意力等评估缺口，强调填补这些缺口对衡量多模态智能真实进展的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型能力快速提升，但评估方法未能同步发展，导致难以衡量模型是否真正实现了跨模态信息整合，需要识别现有评估体系的不足并推动改进。

Method: 通过审视当前多模态模型评估手段，梳理现有基准测试分类法，系统性识别评估中的空白与不足。

Result: 识别出四类关键评估缺口：时空连贯性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意力。

Conclusion: 填补这些评估缺口对于衡量多模态智能的真实进展、揭示模型能力边界至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [97] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym是一个统一的量化金融智能体开发环境，覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等多个任务，提供自动化任务构建、容器化运行时、回测引擎等功能，支持SFT和RL后训练集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估平台碎片化，仅关注单一任务，夸大智能体能力，无法揭示其在泛化、真实市场交互和金融意义决策上的不足，而金融工作流本质上是多阶段的。

Method: 提出OpenFinGym，包含：(1)统一执行验证接口覆盖多任务；(2)自动化任务构建流水线将量化金融论文转化为可执行任务；(3)容器化运行时+主机端验证器(防止训练测试泄露)；(4)低延迟数据流回测引擎；(5)长周期事件市场预测的延迟结算支持；(6)SFT和RL后训练集成。

Result: OpenFinGym提供了一个涵盖预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测的量化金融智能体开发与评估的统一平台。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym通过统一多任务评估、自动化任务构建和防泄露机制，弥补了现有平台碎片化的不足，更真实地评估智能体在量化金融中的综合能力。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [98] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了"组合行为泄露"（CBL）概念，即提示组合代理系统中一个模块的编辑会静默改变其他模块的行为。通过一个三通道协议在部署的岗位评估代理上实验，发现仅内容通道产生可检测的成对效应（Cohen's d=0.63），虽未导致推荐翻转，但在数千次决策中会累积影响。CBL与已知的代理失效维度正交，论文提供了操作定义、可复用协议、可证伪预测集和系统类特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示组合代理系统的实践者报告了一个反复出现的失败模式：编辑一个提示模块会静默地改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们之间没有共享变量或可执行依赖。这种跨模块干扰缺乏正式的术语定义和系统化测量方法，亟需建立理论框架和评估协议。

Method: 通过一个可复用的三通道协议进行实验：在部署的岗位评估代理（Claude Sonnet 4.6，144次试验）上，沿体积（volume）、内容（content）和形式（form）三个通道对非焦点模块进行扰动，测量焦点模块的行为变化。

Result: 仅内容通道产生可检测的成对效应（Cohen's d=0.63，bootstrap 95% CI排除零值）；没有推荐发生翻转——即处于亚阈值区间，标准QA无法检测，但在代理数千次决策中会累积放大。

Conclusion: CBL与已知的代理失效维度（对抗注入、认知退化、多代理故障传播、隐私泄露）正交。论文提供了操作定义、可复用协议、可证伪预测集和系统类特征，将跨模块干扰测量确立为提示组合代理评估的必要条件。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [99] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出库兹韦尔的加速回报理论描述了技术与算力的指数级增长，但即使这种加速真实存在，也无法解决科学发现的核心问题——即对当前框架结构性不足的定性推理能力。论文以ARC-AGI-3基准测试结果（人类近乎满分，AI低于1%）为例，说明当前AI与人类灵活推理间的巨大差距，并提出定性引擎（QES）作为弥补这一缺失能力的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库兹韦尔的加速回报理论是技术发展讨论中最具影响力的叙事之一，但其核心主张——技术进步自我放大并呈指数级增长——是否真正解决了科学发现的核心问题，尚缺乏深入分析。本文旨在澄清该理论的适用范围与局限。

Method: 本文首先对库兹韦尔加速回报理论进行简明的数学诠释，然后通过逻辑论证指出该理论主要适用于执行性与基础设施能力，而非科学发现所需的定性推理能力。以ARC-AGI-3基准测试结果为实证，并引入QES作为应对方案。

Result: 加速回报理论无法解决科学发现中关于"何时现有框架结构上不足"以及"下一步需要什么概念性转变"的定性推理问题。ARC-AGI-3结果（人类天花板性能 vs. 前沿AI <1%）显化了这一鸿沟。

Conclusion: 加速回报理论解释了定量能力的加速机制，但科学发现的核心问题需要QES这类关注定性推理的框架来解决。QES的价值不依赖于AGI何时到来，而在于科学发现过程本身就是一种值得保存、组织和传承的人类智慧。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.

</details>


### [100] [Narration-of-Thought: Inference-Time Scaffolding for Defeasible Ethical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26366)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出叙述性思维（NoT）提示框架，通过结构化思维链为五个强制步骤来缓解道德困境中的利益相关者崩溃和不确定性抑制问题；无需训练或微调即可显著提升模型输出的完整性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链在处理道德困境时存在两个关键失败模式：利益相关者崩溃（最多只提及一方利益相关者）和不确定性抑制（在承诺行动前缺乏明确的未知或保留表述），这导致决策过程缺乏全面的伦理考量。

Method: 设计了NoT系统提示，将思维链强制划分为五个部分：主角、利益相关者、两步后果、不确定性和承诺。采用消融实验验证各部分贡献，并通过文本梯度下降进一步优化提示框架。此外，扩展到五轮多利益相关者辩论协议。

Result: 在100个DailyDilemmas场景、四个生成器上的实验显示：利益相关者崩溃从最高31%降至低于1%，不确定性抑制从最高72%降至1-24%。匹配预算的冗长思维链对照排除了token数量作为混淆变量。NoT在利益相关者数量上保持Cliff's delta优势+0.79至+0.90，不确定性得分上+0.65至+0.93。跨家族训练评判器在所有指标上优于同家族评判器。五轮辩论协议将6%的僵局转化为95%的完全共识。

Conclusion: NoT提供了一种零训练、零参数的方法来改善LLM在道德推理中的全面性和透明度，生成的推理轨迹外部化了支撑每个承诺的利益相关者、后果和不确定性，为可审计的自主代理部署提供了可行基础。

Abstract: Standard chain-of-thought on moral dilemmas exhibits two failure modes: stakeholder collapse (the trace names at most one party with a stake in the outcome) and uncertainty suppression (no explicit unknowns or hedges before committing to an action). We introduce narration-of-thought (NoT), a system prompt that structures chain-of-thought into five sections: protagonist, stakeholders, two-step consequences, uncertainty, then commitment. NoT adds no training, parameters, or fine-tuning. On 100 DailyDilemmas scenarios across four generators from three vendors, NoT cuts stakeholder collapse from up to 31% to under 1% and uncertainty suppression from up to 72% to 1-24% on every model. A matched-budget verbose-CoT control rules out token spend as the active ingredient; NoT retains Cliff's delta advantages of +0.79 to +0.90 on stakeholder count and +0.65 to +0.93 on uncertainty score for three of four generators, and a section ablation attributes each shift to its specific sub-instruction. Textual-gradient descent initialised at NoT improves the scaffold further; a cross-family training judge (different vendor from the generator) dominates an in-family one on every measured axis. Extended to a five-round multi-stakeholder debate protocol, the scaffold converts a 6% standoff into 95% full consensus on a calibration set and 100% combined convergence on a DailyDilemmas replication. The resulting traces externalise the stakeholders, consequences, and uncertainty grounding each commitment, providing an auditable substrate for dependable agentic deployment.

</details>


### [101] [Geometry-Aware MCTS for Extremal Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26399)
*Luoning Zhang,Xu Zhuang,Tianhao Wang,Nathan Kaplan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）框架，用于解决组合几何中的极值问题。该框架通过增量更新可行动作空间严格强制几何约束，利用几何对称性进行剪枝和批量状态转移，显著提升了搜索效率。在Max-N3IL问题上，对于82≤n≤119的网格，找到了约1.8n大小的配置；在最小完全集问题上，找到了约0.95n大小的配置，在六个测试问题中的五个上取得了新的最佳计算结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合几何中的极值问题（如No-Three-in-Line问题）存在严格的全局几何约束，经典精确求解器受组合爆炸困扰，标准强化学习和Transformer模型则面临稀疏奖励"有效性悬崖"和二次令牌消耗限制等瓶颈。需要一种能够有效处理几何约束的新型搜索框架。

Method: 提出Geometry-Aware MCTS框架：(1) 通过增量更新可行动作空间严格强制几何约束，将共线点约束的检查复杂度从O(n³)降至O(n²)；(2) 利用几何对称性进行规范化剪枝以降低分支因子；(3) 采用对称批量状态转移加速发现 promising 配置。

Result: 在六个测试问题中的五个上取得了新的最佳计算结果。Max-N3IL问题上，对于82≤n≤119的网格找到了约1.8n大小的配置。最小完全集问题上，找到了约0.95n大小的配置，提供了新的上界。

Conclusion: Geometry-Aware MCTS是一种高度自适应的框架，能够有效发现组合几何中的新颖配置。

Abstract: We study certain extremal problems in combinatorial geometry that ask about configurations of points in an $n \times n$ grid that satisfy strict, global geometric constraints. Classical exact solvers suffer from combinatorial explosion for these types of problems, and standard reinforcement learning and transformer-based models struggle with the sparse reward "validity cliff" and quadratic token-consumption limits. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a Geometry-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) framework. Our approach strictly enforces geometric constraints through incremental updates to the feasible action space. For constraints about collections of collinear points, like those that occur in the classic No-Three-in-Line problem (Max-N3IL), this mechanism reduces the constraint checking complexity from $O(n^3)$ to $O(n^2)$. To improve search efficiency, we exploit geometric symmetries in two ways: canonical pruning during node expansion to reduce the branching factor, and symmetric batch transitions to accelerate the discovery of promising configurations. We perform extensive experiments and establish new best-known computational results on five out of six of the problems that we considered. Notably, for Max-N3IL we find configurations of size roughly $1.8 n$ for grids of size $82 \le n \le 119$. For the Smallest Complete Set problem, we find configurations of size roughly $0.95 n$, providing new upper bounds within the tested grids. This work establishes Geometry-Aware MCTS as a highly adaptable framework for discovering novel configurations in combinatorial geometry.

</details>


### [102] [When Agents Meet Electric Bus Fleet Operations: Pricing Behavior, Trade-offs, and Policy Implications in an Aggregator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26400)
*Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Ali Eslami,Luis Miranda-Moreno,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种智能体聚合框架，将基于优化的电动公交调度模型与监督智能体（用于扰动检测、电价自适应和调度评估）相结合，以协调电动公交车队在V2G场景下的复杂运营。研究表明，该框架能在保持可行调度的同时选择性触发再优化，但利润导向的配置可能从公交运营商处提取价值，需透明协调与显式价值共享规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动公交车队的运营需要在服务可靠性、电池荷电状态、充电桩可用性、电价、路线能耗不确定性及V2G机会之间持续协调，现有方法难以高效应对这一复杂决策环境。

Method: 提出智能体聚合器框架，耦合基于优化的电动公交调度模型（核心求解器，保障物理可行性）与三个监督智能体层（扰动检测、电价自适应、调度评估），在日前和实时运行中根据条件变化触发再优化，并定义灵活性价值在聚合器与公交运营商之间的分配方式。

Result: 框架能够维持可行调度、选择性激活再优化、提升充电与V2G灵活性利用。但利润导向的协调模式下，智能体能力可能在降低运营复杂度的同时从公交运营商处提取价值。

Conclusion: 智能体聚合器能有效管理电动公交V2G运营，但在公共车队场景中需要透明协调模式、可审计的电价设定和显式的价值共享规则。

Abstract: Agentic systems are changing how complex operational tasks are coordinated, introducing a new paradigm for connecting heterogeneous data sources and automating processes. Electric bus fleets provide a relevant test case. Their operation requires continuous coordination between service reliability, battery state-of-charge, charger availability, electricity prices, route-energy uncertainty, and vehicle-to-grid (V2G) opportunities. This paper proposes an agentic aggregator framework that streamlines this decision environment by coupling an optimization-based electric bus scheduling model with supervisory agents for disturbance detection, tariff adaptation, and schedule evaluation. The optimization core enforces physical feasibility across routes, chargers, batteries, and V2G exchanges, while the agentic layer interprets changing operating conditions, triggers real-time re-optimization when needed, and defines how flexibility value is allocated between the aggregator and the public transport operator (PTO). A realistic depot case study evaluates day-ahead and real-time operations under profit-based and operation-based coordination modes, considering service delays, route-energy deviations, electricity price shocks, and combined disturbances. The results show that agentic aggregation can support adaptive fleet-grid coordination by maintaining feasible schedules, activating re-optimization selectively, and improving the use of charging and V2G flexibility. However, they also reveal a critical trade-off: the same agentic capability that reduces operational complexity can extract value from the PTO when configured around profit-oriented pricing. These findings suggest that agentic aggregators can become useful for managing electric bus V2G operations, but their deployment in public-fleet contexts requires transparent coordination modes, auditable tariff-setting, and explicit value-sharing rules.

</details>


### [103] [Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418)
*Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论了非智能体"预言机"AI在估计未来事件概率时面临的自我指涉问题：一旦答案被知晓并采取行动，就会改变原本要报告的概率。现有方案（科学家AI项目）仅回答反事实问题，但该答案在被知晓时即失效。作者提出一种自我指涉的替代方案：预言机报告的不是单一概率，而是一个同时无偏且与"被学习"的后果自洽的信度集。利用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理在封闭信度集完备格上取最小不动点，证明存在性、自洽性和非空性，并证明在非执行性问题上退化为经典点估计，在二元事件下规范答案为区间。该框架从二元事件推广到任意随机变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非智能体AI预测中的自我指涉悖论：预言机输出概率后，该信息被学习并影响行为，从而改变事件原本的概率分布，导致预测失效。现有反事实方案（假设答案无影响）在答案被知晓时前提不成立，因此需要一种学习后仍自洽的替代方案。

Method: 将预言机输出从单一点概率扩展为信度集（credal set），要求该集同时满足无偏性和自洽性（学习答案后不变）。利用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理，在封闭信度集的完备格上定义保序算子，取最小不动点作为规范解。另有一种变体取包含所有自洽点估计的最小不动点。证明存在性、自洽性、非空性，以及非执行性问题下退化为经典点估计。在二元事件中，在自然凸包分解假设下规范答案为区间。整个框架从二元事件推广到任意随机变量。

Result: 1. 证明了最小不动点的存在性、自洽性和非空性；2. 证明了对非执行性问题（答案不影响事件概率），该构造退化为经典点估计；3. 在二元事件下，在自然凸包分解假设下规范答案为区间；4. 从二元事件B推广到任意随机变量X，用条件律$\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$替代$P(B\mid A,C)$。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于格论不动点定理的解决方案，用自洽信度集替代单一点概率来应对AI预言机的自我指涉问题。该方案在理论上具有良好性质，但区间特征化能否在一般随机变量下保持仍是开放问题。

Abstract: A non-agentic "oracle" AI that estimates probabilities of future events faces a self-reference problem: once its answer is learned and acted upon, it can change the very probability it was asked to report. One response, advocated for the Scientist AI programme, is to ask only counterfactual questions, evaluated as if the answer had no influence. We observe that such answers tend to become irrelevant the moment they are learned, precisely because their premise is then false. We therefore explore a self-referential alternative in which the oracle reports not a single probability but a credal set that is simultaneously unbiased and self-consistent with the consequences of being learned. The naive self-consistency requirement is satisfied by too many sets (including the useless answer $[0,1]$), so the problem is to single out a canonical, nontrivial member. We do so with the Knaster--Tarski fixed-point theorem on the complete lattice of closed credal sets, taking the least fixed point of a suitably defined isotone operator; a variant instead reports the least fixed point that contains every self-consistent point estimate. We prove existence, self-consistency, and nonemptiness, show that the construction collapses to the classical point answer for non-performative questions, and that for a binary event the canonical answer is, under a natural hull-factoring assumption, an interval. The development is purely lattice-theoretic and extends unchanged from a binary event $B$ to an arbitrary random variable $X$, with $P(B\mid A,C)$ replaced by the conditional law $\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$. We close with open questions, including whether the interval characterization itself survives that generalization.

</details>


### [104] [Estimating Uncertainty in Classifier Performance with Applications to Large Language Models and Nested Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26422)
*Kylie Anglin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了社会科学文本分类场景下多种置信区间估计方法的表现，发现Wald区间和基本百分位bootstrap在不频繁构念、小样本和嵌套文本条件下覆盖精度不足，推荐使用Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson区间以及一种新颖的伪计数正则化bootstrap，并强调在嵌套数据中需调整有效样本量和自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本分类中用于衡量构念的性能指标（如召回率、精确率）是点估计，存在抽样变异，但学界对不确定性度量报告不一致且方法不当，尤其在小标注样本和高性能场景下常用方法不适用。

Method: 通过模拟实验，系统比较了多种置信区间方法（Wald、Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson、基本百分位bootstrap、伪计数正则化bootstrap、层次bootstrap、聚类bootstrap）在社会科学典型条件（小到中等样本量、不频繁构念、文本嵌套于个体）下的表现。

Result: 默认方法（Wald区间、基本百分位bootstrap）最不准确，覆盖有时远低于名义95%水平；Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson及所提出的伪计数正则化bootstrap（尤其适用于F1）精度更高；嵌套数据下需同时调整有效N和自由度；层次bootstrap在个体生成中等数量文本时优于聚类bootstrap，但个体仅产生少量文本时过于保守。

Conclusion: 为提升机器学习应用的透明度，研究者应选用更合适的置信区间估计方法，并在设计阶段更加关注验证样本量。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use text classification--supervised models or large language models--to measure constructs from natural language, providing metrics such as recall and precision as evidence of their validity. Yet, though these metrics are point estimates subject to sampling variation, measures of uncertainty are inconsistently reported alongside them. Further, when they are reported, they are often estimated with methods that are not appropriate when relevant labelled datasets are small or performance is high. To increase and improve confidence interval reporting in the field, this paper evaluates confidence interval methods for performance metrics under conditions typical of social science text classification: small to moderate sample sizes, infrequent constructs, and texts nested within individuals. Across simulations, default methods such as the Wald interval and the basic percentile bootstrap are the least accurate, with coverage sometimes far below the nominal 95% level. Accuracy is improved with the use of Agresti-Coull, Wilson, Clopper-Pearson, and a novel pseudo-count regularized bootstrap (which is particularly relevant to the calculation of F1). When texts are nested within individuals, we demonstrate that adjustment for both effective N and the appropriate degrees of freedom is necessary for producing accurate analytic intervals. Among bootstrap intervals, the hierarchical bootstrap is more accurate than the cluster bootstrap when individuals produce a moderate number of texts but overly conservative when individuals produce only a few. By providing guidance to the field on appropriate interval estimation, we aim to improve the transparency of machine learning applications, and to encourage greater attention to the validation sample size at the design stage.

</details>


### [105] [Data-driven Machine Learning Cannot Reach Symbolic-level Logical Reasoning -- The Limit of the Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26454)
*Tiansi Dong,Mateja Jamnik,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了监督式深度学习系统由于方法论上的两个根本性局限（训练数据无法区分所有24种有效三段论推理类型，以及端到端映射导致模式识别与逻辑推理的神经组件之间存在矛盾训练目标），无法达到符号级三段论推理的严格性；实验表明Euler Net无法实现严格三段论推理，ChatGPT在四种表面形式下表现不稳定且即使准确率达100%仍会给出错误解释，因此结论是监督式机器学习无法达到符号逻辑推理的严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究数据驱动机器学习系统能否通过增加训练数据和训练时间达到符号级三段论推理，即逻辑推理中缩放定律的极限在哪里。

Method: 理论分析揭示了监督式深度学习的两个方法论局限；实验验证Euler Net无法实现严格三段论推理；测试ChatGPT（GPT-5-nano和GPT-5）在四种表面形式（单词、双词、简单符号、长随机符号）下的三段论可满足性判断表现。

Result: 监督式深度学习无法达到符号级三段论推理的严谨性；表面形式影响ChatGPT的推理性能；GPT-5虽可达100%准确率但仍会提供错误解释。

Conclusion: 由于训练过程在达到100%准确率后即停止，监督式机器学习系统无法达到符号逻辑推理的严谨性。

Abstract: Sphere neural networks have achieved symbolic level syllogistic reasoning without training data, raising the question of where the limit of the scaling law for logical reasoning lies, i.e., whether data-driven machine learning systems can achieve the same level by increasing training data and training time. We show two methodological limitations that prevent supervised deep learning from reaching the symbolic-level syllogistic reasoning: (1) training data can not distinguish all 24 types of valid syllogistic reasoning; (2) end-to-end mapping from premises to conclusion introduces contradictory training targets between neural components for pattern recognition and logical reasoning. Beside theoretical analysis, we experimentally illustrate that Euler Net cannot achieve rigorous syllogistic reasoning. We further challenge the most recent ChatGPTs (GPT-5-nano and GPT-5) to determine the satisfiability of syllogistic statements in four surface forms (patterns): words, double words, simple symbols, and long random symbols, showing that surface forms affect the reasoning performance and that ChatGPT GPT-5 may reach 100% accuracy but still provide incorrect explanations. As empirical training processes are stopped after achieving 100% accuracy, we conclude that supervised machine learning systems will not attain the rigour of symbolic logical reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [MKG-RAG-Bench: Benchmarking Retrieval in Multimodal Knowledge Graph-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26458)
*Xiaochen Wang,Bao Hoang,Han Liu,Ting Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MKG-RAG-Bench，一个专门用于评估多模态知识图谱检索增强生成中检索环节的跨领域基准，揭示了多模态检索仍然是MKG-RAG系统的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准忽视了多模态知识图谱RAG（MKG-RAG）中检索环节的挑战，多模态知识的异构性、跨模态对齐困难以及现有检索器在非结构化语料上的局限使得检索成为关键瓶颈。

Method: 基于通用和医学两个领域的多模态知识图谱构建基准，采用LLM驱动的策展流程过滤低效用知识、生成结构化的带精确监督的查询，并系统覆盖多种模态配置。

Result: 实验表明有效多模态检索仍然具有挑战性但对端到端MKG-RAG性能至关重要，检索质量强烈决定生成结果。

Conclusion: MKG-RAG-Bench将检索作为一等评估目标，为诊断当前局限和推进多模态知识图谱RAG系统提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over knowledge graphs has emerged as a promising approach for grounding large language models, yet existing benchmarks largely overlook the challenges of retrieval in multimodal knowledge graph RAG (MKG-RAG). In practice, retrieval is a critical bottleneck: multimodal knowledge is heterogeneous, difficult to align across modalities, and often poorly served by retrievers designed for unstructured corpora. To address this gap, we introduce MKG-RAG-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate retrieval in MKG-RAG. MKG-RAG-Bench is constructed from two multimodal knowledge graphs spanning general and medical domains, and includes carefully aligned question-answering datasets that support controlled evaluation of both retrieval and downstream generation. The benchmark is built using an LLM-based curation pipeline that filters low-utility knowledge, generates structurally grounded queries with exact supervision, and systematically covers diverse modality configurations. Through extensive experiments across representative retriever families and modality settings, we show that effective multimodal retrieval remains challenging yet crucial for end-to-end MKG-RAG performance, and that retrieval quality strongly determines generation outcomes. By isolating retrieval as a first-class evaluation target, MKG-RAG-Bench provides a principled foundation for diagnosing current limitations and advancing multimodal knowledge graph RAG systems.

</details>


### [107] [auto-psych: Automating the science of mind using agent-driven theory discovery and experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26460)
*Ben Prystawski,Kushin Mukherjee,Daniel Wurgaft,Linas Nasvytis,Michael Y. Li,Noah D. Goodman,Michael C. Frank*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出auto-psych系统，通过嵌套式Agent发现循环，自动收集众包人类数据并生成认知科学理论，实验证明该系统能有效恢复真实理论并优于文献中的既有理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学与计算认知科学中，理论常以代码形式表示，且众包平台可实现规模化程序化人类数据收集，但数据收集仍是自动化科学发现的主要瓶颈，亟需自动化实验与理论发现的方法。

Method: 构建auto-psych系统，采用嵌套式Agent发现循环：内循环负责猜想、拟合和批判概率认知模型；外循环设计实验、在线发布并分析数据。以硬币抛掷序列随机性判断这一经典认知心理学案例为测试平台。

Result: 该系统能从合成数据中快速可靠地恢复真实理论，嵌套结构对模型性能至关重要；在三轮独立人类实验中，系统发现的理论比文献中已有理论更好地拟合了实际人类行为数据。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在计算认知科学中实现自动化数据收集与理论发现的可行性，为AI驱动的科学自动化提供了新范式。

Abstract: AI-based scientific automation is increasingly possible by using agents to generate hypotheses, design experiments, and analyze data. Data collection is a major bottleneck in this pipeline, however. Psychology, and computational cognitive science in particular, is well-positioned to benefit from AI experimentation because theories are often represented as code and crowdsourcing platforms enable programmatic human data collection at scale. Here, we apply automated discovery techniques to the project of generating theories in computational cognitive science, with an agent-based system collecting human data independently through crowdsourced survey experiments. As a testbed, we use a classic case study from cognitive psychology: judging which sequences of coin flips seem subjectively more random. Our system, auto-psych, uses nested agent-based discovery loops to generate explanatory theories of human behavior. The inner loop conjectures, fits, and critiques probabilistic cognitive models; the outer loop designs experiments to test these models, launches them online, and analyzes the data. This system can quickly and reliably recover ground-truth theories from synthetic data via systematic experimentation, but the nested structure is critical to model performance. Further, in three independent sequences of human experiments, the system finds theories that fit the data better than theories generated from the scientific literature. This work thus demonstrates the feasibility of automated data collection and theory discovery in computational cognitive science.

</details>


### [108] [Clinical Harness for Governable Medical AI Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26494)
*Tianhan Xu,Lei Bao,Yongxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出临床AI技能（Clinical AI Skills）和临床束（Clinical Harness）架构，用于注册、编排、守护和监控AI驱动的临床能力，并以骨质疏松为例展示了知识驱动、数据驱动和物理增强技能在生命周期护理中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗AI仍以孤立模型为主，而临床护理需要可跨时间持续、可问责的稳定能力，因此需要一种运行时治理架构来整合和管理AI临床能力。

Method: 提出临床束（Clinical Harness）运行时治理架构，支持AI临床技能的注册、编排、守护和监控，并通过骨质疏松案例整合知识驱动、数据驱动和物理增强三种技能。

Result: 展示了临床束架构能够支持多种AI技能在生命周期护理中的协同运行与运行时治理。

Conclusion: 临床AI技能与临床束架构为从孤立模型向可持续、可问责的临床AI能力转变提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Medical AI remains organized around isolated models, whereas clinical care requires accountable capabilities that persist across time. We propose clinical AI skills and the Clinical Harness: a runtime governance architecture for registering, orchestrating, guarding and monitoring AI-enabled clinical capabilities. Using osteoporosis as an exemplar, we show how knowledge-driven, data-driven and physics-enhanced skills can support lifecycle care under runtime governance.

</details>


### [109] [Humans Disengage, Reasoning Models Persist: Separating Difficulty Registration from Deliberation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26502)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型推理模型（LRMs）在难题上花费更多token，但同一题目答错时比答对时消耗更多token；人类则相反，答错时用时更短。两者在跨题难度追踪上一致（注册效应），但在题内资源分配上完全相反：LRMs存在显著的“错-对”效应（Cohen's d=1.47-3.13），而人类呈反向模式。这一分离在控制题目固定效应后仍成立，揭示了两种不同的停止策略：人类表现为“参与vs放弃”，LRMs表现为“不确定性驱动长度”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅关注推理时间与难度之间的跨题相关性，忽略了同一题目内正确与错误试次之间的资源分配差异。作者发现LRMs与人类在题内分配模式上完全相反，这一分离被以往研究忽略，需要系统揭示并解释其背后不同的元推理策略。

Method: 在人类-LRM配对语料库上，分离了两种水平的推理时间分析：跨题的注册效应（难度追踪）和题内的分配效应（成败比较）。使用固定效应模型控制题目身份，计算Cohen's d量化LRM与人类的错-对效应差异，并在多个数据集上复现，同时引入非思考基线模型进行对照。

Result: 所有5个思考型LRM在跨题层面与人类一致（注册效应），但在题内层面呈现反向模式：LRM答错时token数显著多于答对时（Cohen's d=1.47-3.13），而人类答错时用时更短。该分离在控制题目固定效应后仍然稳健，在多个数据集上复现，且不存在于非思考基线模型中。

Conclusion: 人类模式反映“参与vs放弃”策略——对预期能解的题持续投入，对难题直接放弃；LRM模式反映“不确定性驱动长度”——模型越不确定（越可能失败）链越长。两种策略共享相同的跨题难度信号，因此仅看跨题相关性会掩盖本质差异。在资源理性元推理框架下，这一分离对应两种拥有相同信号但执行相反控制的停止策略。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) take longer on harder problems, just as humans do. This surface similarity hides an opposite pattern within items. When an LRM gets a problem wrong, it spends more tokens than when it gets the same problem right; humans do the reverse, spending less time on the trials they get wrong. We separate two levels of deliberation: how response time tracks difficulty across items (registration), and, with item identity held fixed, whether an agent spends more on its own failures or successes (allocation). On a public matched human-LRM corpus, humans and all five thinking LRMs reproduce the known cross-item alignment (registration) but diverge within items (allocation): every LRM shows a large wrong-vs-right effect (Cohen's d = 1.47-3.13 on H-ARC) while humans show the opposite sign. The comparison stays inside each agent's own scale; we never put seconds and tokens on one axis. The dissociation holds under item fixed effects, replicates across datasets, and is absent in a non-thinking baseline. We read the human pattern as engagement versus abandonment: people stay on items they expect to solve and give up on the rest. We read the LRM pattern as length driven by uncertainty: chains grow when the model is unsure, which is exactly when it tends to fail. Both policies produce the same cross-item correlation with difficulty, so they look aligned on the measure prior work has used; the divergence shows up only once item identity is fixed. Under resource-rational metareasoning, the split is between two stopping policies that share a difficulty signal but implement opposite control; trace length captures the signal and misses the control.

</details>


### [110] [NeuraDock Visual Cognitive Load Agent Tutorial: A Quality-Gated Open-Source EEG Workflow for Alpha Dynamics and Real-Time Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26518)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Yueqing Dai,Junling Li,Junwen Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NeuraDock Agent——一个面向Alpha动力学和视觉认知负荷分析的开源EEG智能体，提供了从安装部署、预处理质控、Alpha特征提取、在线仪表盘到实时API调用和LLM解释层的完整可复现教程，并通过小型数据集验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG工具包虽然提供优秀的离线分析能力，但构建一个实时、质量门控的认知负荷流水线通常需要手动拼接采集、自定义质控、Alpha特征提取和Web API等多个环节，缺乏从离线到在线的一体化解决方案。

Method: 采用质量门控工作流，在预处理和质控通过后才计算下游Alpha和工作量指标，而非直接从原始EEG计算；提供从安装、预处理、Alpha动力学可视化、被试内静息态/任务态对比、小数据集验证、在线仪表盘启动、实时API调用到LLM质量风险解释层的完整步骤。

Result: 在小数据集验证中，处理了18个记录，生成了10个被试内对比，其中7个观察到任务相关的后部Alpha抑制，初步评估了被试内可重复性，并对本地在线API延迟进行了基准测试。

Conclusion: 该教程面向研究者、开发者和应用团队，提供了一条从EEG文件到实时视觉认知负荷原型的透明路径，弥合了离线分析与在线部署之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This tutorial paper provides a step-by-step, reproducible walkthrough of NeuraDock Agent, an open-source EEG agent focused on Alpha dynamics and visual cognitive-load analysis. The goal is practical: a reader should be able to install the agent, run EEG preprocessing and quality control, generate Alpha dynamics figures, perform within-subject Rest/Task visual cognitive-load comparison, run the public mini-dataset analyses and compare them with the reference validation summary, start an online dashboard, call the real-time API from an external application, and use the LLM interpretation layer to explain quality risks. Existing EEG toolkits provide excellent offline analysis, but assembling a real-time, quality-gated cognitive-load pipeline often requires manually bridging acquisition, custom QC, Alpha feature extraction, and a web API; this tutorial closes that offline-to-online gap. The tutorial uses a quality-gated workflow: downstream Alpha and workload metrics are computed only after preprocessing and QC gating rather than directly from raw EEG. In the included mini-dataset validation, the agent processed 18 recordings, generated 10 within-subject comparisons, observed task-related posterior Alpha suppression in 7 of 10 contrasts, estimated initial evidence of within-subject repeatability, and benchmarked local online API latency. The tutorial is intended for researchers, developers, and applied teams who want a transparent path from EEG files to real-time visual cognitive-load prototypes.

</details>


### [111] [Boundary-Aware Context Grounding for A Low-Channel EEG Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26519)
*Zhiyuan Xu,Yueqing Dai,Junling Li,Junwen Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuraDock Agent 是一个开源架构，通过将确定性本地EEG引擎与硬件感知语言层分离，实现了LLM在低通道脑电图分析中的硬件感知和实现边界校准，在数值重复性、故障容错和边界感知基准测试中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型不了解特定传感器的测量能力、当前软件实现的算法以及计算结果所能支持的结论。这一问题在低通道脑电图（EEG）中尤为突出，因为稀疏的空间覆盖和可变的信号质量使得看似合理但实际上缺乏支持的解读容易被生成。

Method: 提出NeuraDock Agent开源架构，将确定性本地EEG引擎与硬件感知语言层分离。数值引擎负责解析记录、质量控制、执行频谱工作流并生成机器可读结果；LLM仅接收经过允许列表压缩的摘要和版本化的上下文包，原始EEG和密集数据保留在本地。

Result: 三层评估结果：（1）12个记录在10次数值重复中产生相同结果；（2）请求捕获和故障注入实验验证了数据边界和本地工件保护；（3）边界感知基准测试在4种上下文消融和2种LLM下测试36个问题，共288个输出。

Conclusion: 硬件感知和实现感知的接地机制是校准EEG代理接受、限定或拒绝内容的实用方法，但未建立临床有效性或验证的绝对认知负荷指数。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can make scientific software easier to use. However, a general model does not automatically know which measurements a particular sensor can support, which algorithms are implemented in the current software, or which conclusions are justified by a computed result. These distinctions are especially important for low-channel electroencephalography (EEG), where sparse spatial coverage and variable signal quality make plausible but unsupported interpretations easy to produce. We present NeuraDock Agent, an open-source architecture that separates a deterministic local EEG engine from a hardware-aware language layer. The numerical engine parses recordings, performs quality control, executes reviewed spectral workflows, and writes machine-readable artifacts. The LLM receives only a compact, allowlisted summary and a versioned context pack. The context describes the seven-channel hardware, reviewed workflows, result fields, implementation boundaries, scientific limits, and reference cases. Raw EEG and dense per-sample arrays remain local
  We evaluate the system at three levels. First, 12 recordings produced identical structured results over ten numerical repetitions, and a complete Rest/Task run produced identical result, report, and figure hashes over three repetitions. Second, request-capture and failure-injection experiments confirmed the tested data boundary and preservation of local artifacts under HTTP, malformed-output, and connection failures. Third, a boundary-awareness benchmark tested 36 ordinary and adversarial questions under four context ablations and two LLMs, yielding 288 outputs.These results support hardware- and implementation-aware grounding as a practical mechanism for calibrating what an EEG agent accepts, qualifies, or refuses; they do not establish clinical validity or a validated absolute cognitive-load index.

</details>


### [112] [Radical AI Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26523)
*Daniel A. Herrmann,Benjamin A. Levinstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，结合哲学中的激进诠释传统与机械可解释性工具，将AI系统解释为具有信念、欲望和意义的智能体。核心问题是：给定系统的计算事实，如何求解其信念、欲望和意义？文章提出表征主义和解释主义两种进路的标准，并指出信念、欲望和命题结构必须整体联合约束，不能分而治之。机械可解释性可用于同时测量这些要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统日益强大，理解其内部目标、检测其欺骗行为变得至关重要。然而，当前可解释性研究缺乏一套成熟的评判标准来判断"是否成功读取了模型的信念和欲望"。该研究旨在填补这一理论基础空白。

Method: 融合激进诠释哲学传统（Davidson等）与机械可解释性（Mechanistic Interpretability）技术方法，提出表征主义与解释主义两种进路，并将它们与当前可用的可解释性测试方法挂钩。强调整体论（Holism）约束：信念、欲望与命题结构必须联合求解。

Result: 给出了判定可解释性方法是否成功归因信念和欲望的理论标准；揭示了分而治之（piecemeal）方法的固有偏差——固定一个变量测量另一个会引入扭曲；指出整体论既是挑战（AI系统可能不共享解释者的概念）也是工具（系统的态度约束命题结构，命题结构约束态度归因，机械可解释性可测量两者）。

Conclusion: AI系统的信念、欲望和意义不能孤立地归因，必须通过整体约束联合求解。机械可解释性提供了一种可行的测量手段，能够同时获取系统的态度和命题结构，从而实现更可靠的AI解释与安全评估。

Abstract: We develop a framework for interpreting AI systems as agents, drawing on the philosophical tradition of radical interpretation and the tools of mechanistic interpretability. The core question is: given the computational facts about a system, how do we solve for its beliefs, desires, and meanings? This matters increasingly for safety. We want to be able to trust the systems we deploy, whether by understanding their goals or, more modestly, by reliably detecting deception. Interpretability researchers are building tools to read beliefs and desires off a model's internals, but there is no settled account of when such a tool has succeeded. This book supplies one. We propose criteria on both representationalist and interpretationist approaches, and tie each to tests current interpretability methods can carry out. A central lesson is that these attributions cannot be made piecemeal. Beliefs, desires, and the propositional structure they presuppose are jointly constrained, and a method that fixes one while measuring the others inherits whatever distortions that introduces. This holism becomes pressing for AI systems, which may not share the interpreter's concepts. However, it also provides leverage: a system's attitudes constrain its propositional structure, that structure constrains which attitudes can be attributed, and mechanistic interpretability can help us measure both.

</details>


### [113] [PMDformer: Patch-Mean Decoupling Information Transformer for Long-term Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26549)
*Ao Hu,Liangjian Wen,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,He Yan,Dongkai Wang,Jun Wang,Yukun Zhang,Ruoxi Jiang,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PMDformer模型，通过补丁均值解耦（PMD）、趋势恢复注意力（TRA）和邻近变量注意力（PVA）三大模块，有效解决了长时序列预测中跨补丁和跨变量的形状相似性建模问题，在多个基准上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
